data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/audio.ts","content":"import { WaveformType } from './types';\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n  filterNode: BiquadFilterNode;\n}\n\nclass SoundEngine {\n  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n  private currentWaveform: WaveformType = 'triangle';\n  private currentVolume: number = 0.7;\n\n  private initContext(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.ctx) {\n      const AudioCtxClass = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.ctx = new AudioCtxClass();\n\n      this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.currentVolume, this.ctx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.ctx.resume();\n    }\n\n    return this.ctx;\n  }\n\n  public setVolume(volume: number): void {\n    const clampedVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    this.currentVolume = clampedVolume;\n\n    if (this.ctx && this.masterGain) {\n      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n      this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      this.masterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(clampedVolume, now + 0.05);\n    }\n  }\n\n  public setWaveform(waveform: WaveformType): void {\n    this.currentWaveform = waveform;\n  }\n\n  public playNote(note: string, frequency: number, customWaveform?: WaveformType): void {\n    const ctx = this.initContext();\n    const waveform = customWaveform || this.currentWaveform;\n\n    // 既に同一音階が発音中の場合は一度減衰停止\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(note)) {\n      this.stopNote(note);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    osc.type = waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);\n\n    // 自然なピアノ調の温かみを加えるローパスフィルタ\n    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();\n    filter.type = 'lowpass';\n    const cutoffFreq = Math.min(frequency * 5, 8000);\n    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(cutoffFreq, now);\n    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1, now);\n\n    const gain = ctx.createGain();\n    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n\n    // ADSR エンベロープ設定\n    const attackTime = 0.015;\n    const decayTime = 0.18;\n    const sustainLevel = 0.55;\n\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1.0, now + attackTime);\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(sustainLevel, now + attackTime + decayTime);\n\n    osc.connect(filter);\n    filter.connect(gain);\n    if (this.masterGain) {\n      gain.connect(this.masterGain);\n    }\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(note, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: gain,\n      filterNode: filter,\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(note: string): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(note);\n    if (!voice || !this.ctx) return;\n\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n    const releaseTime = 0.25;\n\n    try {\n      gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001), now);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n      oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.05);\n\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        try {\n          oscillator.disconnect();\n          gainNode.disconnect();\n        } catch {\n          // すでに切断済みの場合は無視\n        }\n      }, (releaseTime + 0.1) * 1000);\n    } catch {\n      try {\n        oscillator.stop();\n      } catch {\n        // 何もしない\n      }\n    }\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(note);\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    const activeKeys = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    for (const key of activeKeys) {\n      this.stopNote(key);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nexport const soundEngine = new SoundEngine();","language":"typescript"}

soundEngine.setWaveform(newWaveform);\n  };\n\n  // 音量変更ハンドラ\n  const handleVolumeChange = (newVolume: number) => {\n    setVolume(newVolume);\n    soundEngine.setVolume(newVolume);\n  };\n\n  // ノート発音処理\n  const handlePlayNote = useCallback((keyItem: PianoKeyItem) => {\n    soundEngine.playNote(keyItem.note, keyItem.frequency);\n\n    setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      next.add(keyItem.note);\n      return next;\n    });\n\n    setLastPlayedNote(keyItem);\n\n    const now = new Date();\n    const timeStr = `${now.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;\n    const newPlayedNote: PlayedNote = {\n      id: `${keyItem.note}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,\n      note: keyItem.note,\n      pitchName: keyItem.pitchName,\n      timestamp: timeStr,\n    };\n\n    setPlayedHistory((prev) => [newPlayedNote, ...prev.slice(0, 11)]);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ノート停止処理\n  const handleStopNote = useCallback((keyItem: PianoKeyItem) => {\n    soundEngine.stopNote(keyItem.note);\n\n    setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      next.delete(keyItem.note);\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  // 全デモ停止\n  const stopDemo = useCallback(() => {\n setIsDemoPlaying(false);\n    setCurrentDemoTitle('');\n  }, []);\n\n  // デモ曲再生\n  const playDemoSong = (song: DemoSongNote[], title: string) => {\n    stopDemo();\n    setIsDemoPlaying(true);\n    setCurrentDemoTitle(title);\n\n    const keyMap = new Map<string, PianoKeyItem>();\n    PIANO_KEYS.forEach((k) => keyMap.set(k.note, k));\n\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    song.forEach((item) => {\n      const targetKey = keyMap.get(item.note);\n      if (!targetKey) return;\n\n      const playTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        handlePlayNote(targetKey);\n      }, item.delay);\n\n      const stopTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        handleStopNote(targetKey);\n      }, item.delay + item.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(playTimeout, stopTimeout);\n\n      if (item.delay + item.duration > maxEndTime) {\n        maxEndTime = item.delay + item.duration;\n      }\n    });\n\n    const finishTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsDemoPlaying(false);\n Map<string, PianoKeyItem>();\n    PIANO_KEYS.forEach((k) => keyMapByBinding.set(k.keyBinding.toLowerCase(), k));\n\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;\n      const key = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = keyMapByBinding.get(key);\n      if (targetKey && !activeNotes.has(targetKey.note)) {\n (targetKey) {\n        handleStopNote(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n\n    return () => {\n htmlFor=\"waveform-select\" className=\"control-label\">\n            音色（波形）\n          </label>\n          <select\n            id=\"waveform-select\"\n            className=\"select-input\"\n            value={waveform}\n            onChange={(e) => handleWaveformChange(e.target.value as WaveformType)}\n          >\n            <option value=\"triangle\">三角波 (柔らかなピアノ風)</option>\n            <option value=\"sine\">サイン波 (澄んだオルガン風)</option>\n            <option value=\"sawtooth\">ノコギリ波 (明るいブラス風)</option>\n            <option value=\"square\">矩形波 (レトロゲーム8bit風)</option>\n          </select>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 音量調整 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"volume-slider\" className=\"control-label\">\n            マスター音量: {Math.round(volume * 100)}%\n          </label>\n          <input\n            id=\"volume-slider\"\n            type=\"range\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            max=\"1\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            value={volume}\n            onChange={(e) => handleVolumeChange(parseFloat(e.target.value))}\n            className=\"slider-input\"\n            aria-label=\"マスター音量\"\n          />\n event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/Keyboard.tsx","content":"import { FC, PointerEvent } from 'react';\nimport { KeyboardProps, PIANO_KEYS, PianoKeyItem } from '../types';\n\nconst Keyboard: FC<KeyboardProps> = ({\n  activeNotes = new Set<string>(),\n  onPlayNote,\n  onStopNote,\n  showKeyLabels = true,\n}) => {\n  const whiteKeys = PIANO_KEYS.filter((k) => !k.isBlack);\n  const blackKeys = PIANO_KEYS.filter((k) => k.isBlack);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (keyItem: PianoKeyItem, e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onPlayNote?.(keyItem);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (keyItem: PianoKeyItem, e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onStopNote?.(keyItem);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerEnter = (keyItem: PianoKeyItem, e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    if (e.buttons > 0) {\n      onPlayNote?.(keyItem);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = (keyItem: PianoKeyItem, _e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    if (activeNotes.has(keyItem.note)) {\n      onStopNote?.(keyItem);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerCancel = (keyItem: PianoKeyItem, e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onStopNote?.(keyItem);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"keyboard-container\">\n      <div className=\"piano-keyboard\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"ピアノ鍵盤\">\n        {/* 白鍵行 */}\n        <div className=\"white-keys-row\">\n          {whiteKeys.map((keyItem) => {\n            const isActive = activeNotes.has(keyItem.note);\n            const className = isActive ? 'white-key key-active' : 'white-key';\n\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={keyItem.note}\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={className}\n                onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointerCancel(keyItem, e)}\n                aria-label={`${keyItem.pitchName} (${keyItem.note})`}\n                aria-pressed={isActive}\n              >\n                {showKeyLabels && (\n                  <div className=\"key-label\">\n                    <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{keyItem.keyBinding.toUpperCase()}</span>\n                    <span className=\"note-name\">{keyItem.pitchName}</span>\n                  </div>\n                )}\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 黒鍵行（CSSのnth-childで配置） */}\n        <div className=\"black-keys-row\">\n          {blackKeys.map((keyItem) => {\n            const isActive = activeNotes.has(keyItem.note);\n            const className = isActive ? 'black-key key-active' : 'black-key';\n\n            return (\n              <button\n                key={keyItem.note}\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={className}\n                onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(keyItem, e)}\n                onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointerCancel(keyItem, e)}\n                aria-label={`${keyItem.pitchName} (${keyItem.note})`}\n                aria-pressed={isActive}\n              >\n                {showKeyLabels && (\n                  <div className=\"key-label\">\n                    <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{keyItem.keyBinding.toUpperCase()}</span>\n                    <span className=\"note-name\">{keyItem.pitchName}</span>\n                  </div>\n                )}\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Keyboard;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/audio.ts","content":"import { WaveformType } from './types';\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n  filterNode: BiquadFilterNode;\n}\n\nconst MAX_POLYPHONY = 16;\n\nclass SoundEngine {\n  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n  private currentWaveform: WaveformType = 'triangle';\n  private currentVolume: number = 0.7;\n\n  private initContext(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.ctx) {\n      const AudioCtxClass =\n        window.AudioContext ||\n        (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.ctx = new AudioCtxClass();\n\n      this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.currentVolume, this.ctx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.ctx.resume().catch((err) => {\n        console.warn('AudioContext resume was prevented or failed:', err);\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.ctx;\n  }\n\n  public setVolume(volume: number): void {\n    const clampedVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    this.currentVolume = clampedVolume;\n\n    if (this.ctx && this.masterGain) {\n      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n      this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      this.masterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(clampedVolume, now + 0.05);\n    }\n  }\n\n  public setWaveform(waveform: WaveformType): void {\n    this.currentWaveform = waveform;\n  }\n\n  public playNote(note: string, frequency: number, customWaveform?: WaveformType): void {\n    const ctx = this.initContext();\n    const waveform = customWaveform || this.currentWaveform;\n\n    // ポリフォニー制限: 上限に達した場合、最も古いボイスを解放\n    if (this.activeVoices.size >= MAX_POLYPHONY && !this.activeVoices.has(note)) {\n      const oldestKey = this.activeVoices.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldestKey) {\n        this.stopNote(oldestKey);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 既に同一音階が発音中の場合は一度減衰停止\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(note)) {\n      this.stopNote(note);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    osc.type = waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);\n\n    // 自然なピアノ調の温かみを加えるローパスフィルタ\n    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();\n    filter.type = 'lowpass';\n    const cutoffFreq = Math.min(frequency * 5, 8000);\n    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(cutoffFreq, now);\n    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1, now);\n\n    const gain = ctx.createGain();\n    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n\n    // ADSR エンベロープ設定\n    const attackTime = 0.015;\n    const decayTime = 0.18;\n    const sustainLevel = 0.55;\n\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1.0, now + attackTime);\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(sustainLevel, now + attackTime + decayTime);\n\n    osc.connect(filter);\n    filter.connect(gain);\n    if (this.masterGain) {\n      gain.connect(this.masterGain);\n    }\n\n    // 再生終了イベントで確実にリソースを切断・解放\n    osc.onended = () => {\n      try {\n        osc.disconnect();\n        filter.disconnect();\n        gain.disconnect();\n      } catch {\n        // すでに切断済みの場合は無視\n      }\n    };\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(note, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: gain,\n      filterNode: filter,\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(note: string): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(note);\n    if (!voice || !this.ctx) return;\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(note);\n\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n    const releaseTime = 0.25;\n\n    try {\n      gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001), now);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n      oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.05);\n    } catch {\n      try {\n        oscillator.stop();\n      } catch {\n        // 何もしない\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    const activeKeys = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    for (const key of activeKeys) {\n      this.stopNote(key);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nexport const soundEngine = new SoundEngine();","language":"typescript"}

from './components/Controls';\nimport {\n  WaveformType,\n  PianoKeyItem,\n  PlayedNote,\n  PIANO_KEYS,\n  DemoSongNote,\n} from './types';\nimport { soundEngine } from './audio';\nimport './index.css';\n\nconst App: FC = () => {\n  const [waveform, setWaveform] = useState<WaveformType>('triangle');\n  const [volume, setVolume] = useState<number>(0.7);\n  const [showKeyLabels, setShowKeyLabels] = useState<boolean>(true);\n  const [activeNotes, setActiveNotes] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [playedHistory, setPlayedHistory] = useState<PlayedNote[]>([]);\n  const [lastPlayedNote, setLastPlayedNote] = useState<PianoKeyItem | null>(null);\n  const [isDemoPlaying, setIsDemoPlaying] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [currentDemoTitle, setCurrentDemoTitle] = useState<string>('');\n\n  const demoTimeoutsRef = useRef<number[]>([]);\n\n  // 音色変更ハンドラ\n  const handleWaveformChange = (newWaveform: WaveformType) => {\n    setWaveform(newWaveform);\n    soundEngine.setWaveform(newWaveform);\n  };\n\n  // 音量変更ハンドラ\n  const handleVolumeChange = (newVolume: number) => {\n    setVolume(newVolume);\n    soundEngine.setVolume(newVolume);\n  };\n\n  // キーラベル表示切り替え\n  const handleToggleKeyLabels = () => {\n    setShowKeyLabels((prev) => !prev);\n  };\n\n  // ノート発音処理\n  const handlePlayNote = useCallback((keyItem: PianoKeyItem) => {\n    soundEngine.playNote(keyItem.note, keyItem.frequency);\n\n    setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      next.add(keyItem.note);\n      return next;\n    });\n\n    setLastPlayedNote(keyItem);\n\n    const now = new Date();\n    const timeStr = `${now.getHours().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${now.getSeconds().toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;\n    const newPlayedNote: PlayedNote = {\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      note: keyItem.note,\n      pitchName: keyItem.pitchName,\n      timestamp: timeStr,\n    };\n\n    setPlayedHistory((prev) => [newPlayedNote, ...prev.slice(0, 11)]);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ノート停止処理\n  const handleStopNote = useCallback((keyItem: PianoKeyItem) => {\n    soundEngine.stopNote(keyItem.note);\n\n    setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      next.delete(keyItem.note);\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  // 全デモ停止\n  const stopDemo = useCallback(() => {\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.forEach((timeoutId) => clearTimeout(timeoutId));\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current = [];\n    soundEngine.stopAllNotes();\n    setActiveNotes(new Set());\n    setIsDemoPlaying(false);\n    setCurrentDemoTitle('');\n  }, []);\n\n  // デモ曲再生\n  const playDemoSong = (song: DemoSongNote[], title: string) => {\n    stopDemo();\n    setIsDemoPlaying(true);\n    setCurrentDemoTitle(title);\n\n    const keyMap = new Map<string, PianoKeyItem>();\n    PIANO_KEYS.forEach((k) => keyMap.set(k.note, k));\n\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    song.forEach((item) => {\n      const targetKey = keyMap.get(item.note);\n      if (!targetKey) return;\n\n      const playTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        handlePlayNote(targetKey);\n      }, item.delay);\n\n      const stopTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        handleStopNote(targetKey);\n      }, item.delay + item.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(playTimeout, stopTimeout);\n\n      if (item.delay + item.duration > maxEndTime) {\n        maxEndTime = item.delay + item.duration;\n      }\n    });\n\n    const finishTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsDemoPlaying(false);\n      setCurrentDemoTitle('');\n    }, maxEndTime + 200);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimeout);\n  };\n\n  // PCキーボード押下イベント\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const keyMapByBinding = new Map<string, PianoKeyItem>();\n    PIANO_KEYS.forEach((k) => keyMapByBinding.set(k.keyBinding.toLowerCase(), k));\n\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;\n      const key = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = keyMapByBinding.get(key);\n      if (targetKey && !activeNotes.has(targetKey.note)) {\n        handlePlayNote(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const key = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = keyMapByBinding.get(key);\n      if (targetKey) {\n        handleStopNote(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n    };\n  }, [activeNotes, handlePlayNote, handleStopNote]);\n\n  // コンポーネント破棄時に音源停止\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      stopDemo();\n    };\n  }, [stopDemo]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* ヘッダー */}\n      <header className=\"header\">\n        <h1 className=\"header-title\">Web Studio Piano</h1>\n        <p className=\"header-subtitle\">\n          Web Audio API搭載・1オクターブ本格演奏ピアノ（PCキーボード＆タッチ対応）\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* コントロールパネル */}\n      <Controls\n        waveform={waveform}\n        volume={volume}\n        showKeyLabels={showKeyLabels}\n        isDemoPlaying={isDemoPlaying}\n        currentDemoTitle={currentDemoTitle}\n        onWaveformChange={handleWaveformChange}\n        onVolumeChange={handleVolumeChange}\n        onToggleKeyLabels={handleToggleKeyLabels}\n        onPlayDemo={playDemoSong}\n        onStopDemo={stopDemo}\n      />\n\n      {/* 鍵盤ユニット */}\n      <Keyboard\n        activeNotes={activeNotes}\n        onPlayNote={handlePlayNote}\n        onStopNote={handleStopNote}\n        showKeyLabels={showKeyLabels}\n      />\n\n      {/* PCキーボード操作案内 */}\n      <div className=\"shortcut-guide\">\n        <strong>PCキーボードショートカット:</strong> 白鍵 [ A S D F G H J K ] ／ 黒鍵 [ W E T Y U ]\n        <br />\n        マウス押下、タッチスライド、複数キー同時押しによる和音演奏にも対応しています。\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 演奏履歴 & 直近の音符 */}\n      <section className=\"history-section\" aria-label=\"演奏履歴\">\n        <div className=\"history-header\">\n          <h2 className=\"history-title\">演奏ログ</h2>\n          {lastPlayedNote && (\n            <span className=\"history-last-note\">\n              直前の音: <strong>{lastPlayedNote.pitchName}</strong> } from './types';\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n  filterNode: BiquadFilterNode;\n}\n\nconst MAX_POLYPHONY = 16;\n\nclass SoundEngine {\n  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n  private currentWaveform: WaveformType = 'triangle';\n  private currentVolume: number = 0.7;\n\n  private initContext(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.ctx) {\n      const AudioCtxClass =\n        window.AudioContext ||\n        (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.ctx = new AudioCtxClass();\n\n      this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(this.currentVolume, this.ctx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.ctx.resume().catch((err) => {\n        console.warn('AudioContext resume was prevented or failed:', err);\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.ctx;\n  }\n\n  public setVolume(volume: number): void {\n    const clampedVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    this.currentVolume = clampedVolume;\n\n    if (this.ctx && this.masterGain) {\n      const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n      this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      this.masterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(clampedVolume, now + 0.05);\n    }\n  }\n\n  public setWaveform(waveform: WaveformType): void {\n    this.currentWaveform = waveform;\n  }\n\n  public playNote(note: string, frequency: number, customWaveform?: WaveformType): void {\n    const ctx = this.initContext();\n    const waveform = customWaveform || this.currentWaveform;\n\n    // ポリフォニー制限: 上限に達した場合、最も古いボイスを解放\n    if (this.activeVoices.size >= MAX_POLYPHONY && !this.activeVoices.has(note)) {\n      const oldestKey = this.activeVoices.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldestKey) {\n        this.stopNote(oldestKey);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 既に同一音階が発音中の場合は一度減衰停止\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(note)) {\n      this.stopNote(note);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    osc.type = waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, now);\n\n    // 自然なピアノ調の温かみを加えるローパスフィルタ\n    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();\n    filter.type = 'lowpass';\n    const cutoffFreq = Math.min(frequency * 5, 8000);\n    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(cutoffFreq, now);\n    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1, now);\n\n    const gain = ctx.createGain();\n    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n\n    // ADSR エンベロープ設定\n    const attackTime = 0.015;\n    const decayTime = 0.18;\n    const sustainLevel = 0.55;\n\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1.0, now + attackTime);\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(sustainLevel, now + attackTime + decayTime);\n\n    osc.connect(filter);\n    filter.connect(gain);\n    if (this.masterGain) {\n      gain.connect(this.masterGain);\n    }\n\n    // 再生終了イベントで確実にリソースを切断・解放\n    osc.onended = () => {\n      try {\n        osc.disconnect();\n        filter.disconnect();\n        gain.disconnect();\n      } catch {\n        // すでに切断済みの場合は無視\n      }\n    };\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(note, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: gain,\n      filterNode: filter,\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(note: string): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(note);\n    if (!voice || !this.ctx) return;\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(note);\n\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n    const releaseTime = 0.25;\n\n    try {\n      gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001), now);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n      oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.05);\n    } catch {\n      try {\n        oscillator.stop();\n      } catch {\n        // 何もしない\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    const activeKeys = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    for (const key of activeKeys) {\n      this.stopNote(key);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nexport const soundEngine = new SoundEngine();","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/Keyboard.tsx","content":"import { FC, PointerEvent } from 'react';\nimport { KeyboardProps, PIANO_KEYS, PianoKeyItem } from onPlayNote?.(keyItem);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (keyItem: PianoKeyItem, e: PointerEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onStopNote?.(keyItem);\n  };\n\n  const PlayedNote,\n  PIANO_KEYS,\n  DemoSongNote,\n} from './types';\nimport { soundEngine } from './audio';\nimport './index.css';\n\nconst App: FC = () => {\n  const [waveform, setWaveform] = 音色変更ハンドラ\n  const handleWaveformChange = (newWaveform: WaveformType) => {\n    setWaveform(newWaveform);\n    soundEngine.setWaveform(newWaveform);\n  };\n\n  // 音量変更ハンドラ\n  const handleVolumeChange = };\n\n  // ノート発音処理\n  const handlePlayNote = useCallback((keyItem: PianoKeyItem) => {\n    soundEngine.playNote(keyItem.note, keyItem.frequency);\n\n    setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      next.add(keyItem.note);\n      return next;\n    });\n\n    setLastPlayedNote(keyItem);\n\n    const now = new Date();\n    const timeStr = handleStopNote(targetKey);\n      }, item.delay + item.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(playTimeout, stopTimeout);\n\n      if (item.delay + item.duration > maxEndTime) {\n    <br />\n        マウス押下、タッチスライド、複数キー同時押しによる和音演奏にも対応しています。\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 演奏履歴 & 直近の音符 */}\n      <section className=\"history-section\" aria-label=\"演奏履歴\">\n        <div
